right;"><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2034_FIFA_World_Cup" title="2034 FIFA World Cup">2034</a></i> <span typeof="mw:Entity">→</span> </div></td></tr></tbody></table><div class="shortdescription nomobile noexcerpt noprint searchaux" style="display:none" about="#mwt6">International football competition</div><span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt6"><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_short_description"/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Short_description_is_different_from_Wikidata"/></span>
<p id="mwGQ">The <b id="mwGg">2030 FIFA World Cup</b> is scheduled to be the 24th <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_World_Cup" title="FIFA World Cup" id="mwGw">FIFA World Cup</a>, the quadrennial international <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Association_football" title="Association football" id="mwHA">football</a> tournament that is contested by the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_men's_national_association_football_teams" title="List of men's national association football teams" id="mwHQ">men's national teams</a> of the member associations of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA" title="FIFA" id="mwHg">FIFA</a>. The tournament is planned to be jointly hosted by Morocco, Portugal, and Spain. In honour of the 100th anniversary of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1930_FIFA_World_Cup" title="1930 FIFA World Cup" id="mwHw">first edition</a> in 1930, a special match and celebration are scheduled to be held at <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Estadio_Centenario" title="Estadio Centenario" id="mwIA">Estadio Centenario</a> in Montevideo, Uruguay—host stadium of the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1930_FIFA_World_Cup_final" title="1930 FIFA World Cup final" id="mwIQ">1930 final</a>—as well as one match each in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Estadio_Monumental_(Buenos_Aires)" title="Estadio Monumental (Buenos Aires)" id="mwIg">Estadio Monumental</a> in Buenos Aires, Argentina, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Estadio_Osvaldo_Domínguez_Dibb" title="Estadio Osvaldo Domínguez Dibb" id="mwIw">Estadio Osvaldo Domínguez Dibb</a> in Asunción, Paraguay.</p>

<p id="mwJA">The tournament will be the first World Cup held in more than one confederation, the first in Africa since <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2010_FIFA_World_Cup" title="2010 FIFA World Cup" id="mwJQ">2010</a>, and the first ever in North Africa. It will be the first held in Morocco, Paraguay, and Portugal, and the second in Uruguay, Argentina, and Spain, after 1930, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1978_FIFA_World_Cup" title="1978 FIFA World Cup" id="mwJg">1978</a>, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1982_FIFA_World_Cup" title="1982 FIFA World Cup" id="mwJw">1982</a> respectively.</p>

<p id="mwKA"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Morocco_national_football_team" title="Morocco national football team" id="mwKQ">Morocco</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Portugal_national_football_team" title="Portugal national football team" id="mwKg">Portugal</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spain_national_football_team" title="Spain national football team" id="mwKw">Spain</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Argentina_national_football_team" title="Argentina national football team" id="mwLA">Argentina</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paraguay_national_football_team" title="Paraguay national football team" id="mwLQ">Paraguay</a>, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Uruguay_national_football_team" title="Uruguay national football team" id="mwLg">Uruguay</a> all automatically qualified for the tournament as hosts, with Spain becoming the first ever <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_FIFA_World_Cup_final" title="2026 FIFA World Cup final" id="mwLw">defending champions</a> to host the tournament after winning their second World Cup in 2026.</p>

<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt20" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwMA" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"TOC limit","href":"./Template:TOC_limit"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"4"}},"i":0}}]}'><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r886046785" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt21" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Template:TOC limit/styles.css"}}'>.mw-parser-output .toclimit-2 .toclevel-1 ul,.mw-parser-output .toclimit-3 .toclevel-2 ul,.mw-parser-output .toclimit-4 .toclevel-3 ul,.mw-parser-output .toclimit-5 .toclevel-4 ul,.mw-parser-output .toclimit-6 .toclevel-5 ul,.mw-parser-output .toclimit-7 .toclevel-6 ul{display:none}</style></span><div class="toclimit-4" about="#mwt20" id="mwMQ"><meta property="mw:PageProp/toc"/></div>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="1" id="mwMg" aria-labelledby="Possible_format_and_expansion"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Possible_format_and_expansion">Possible format and expansion</h2></div>
<p id="mwMw">At the March 2025 <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_Council" title="FIFA Council" id="mwNA">FIFA Council</a> meeting, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CONMEBOL" title="CONMEBOL" id="mwNQ">CONMEBOL</a> proposed a one-off 64-team expansion to honour the tournament's centennial anniversary.<sup about="#mwt24" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-4" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-4"}}'><a href="#cite_note-4" id="mwNg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwNw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwOA">[</span>3<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwOQ">]</span></span></a></sup> Later that year, CONMEBOL leaders met directly with FIFA president <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gianni_Infantino" title="Gianni Infantino" id="mwOg">Gianni Infantino</a> in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_York_City" title="New York City" id="mwOw">New York City</a> to discuss the expansion. In July 2026, Infantino stated that he would consider this expansion.<sup about="#mwt27" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-5" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-5"}}'><a href="#cite_note-5" id="mwPA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwPQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwPg">[</span>4<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwPw">]</span></span></a></sup> On 20 July 2026, CONMEBOL president <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alejandro_Domínguez_(football_executive)" title="Alejandro Domínguez (football executive)" id="mwQA">Alejandro Domínguez</a> announced that the tournament would feature 64 teams in a post on <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/X_(social_network)" title="X (social network)" id="mwQQ">X</a>.<sup about="#mwt30" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-6" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-6"}}'><a href="#cite_note-6" id="mwQg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwQw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRA">[</span>5<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwRQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt33" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-7" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-7"}}'><a href="#cite_note-7" id="mwRg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwRw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSA">[</span>6<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwSQ">]</span></span></a></sup> This has not been confirmed by FIFA. Critics claim increasing the number of qualified teams to 64 would devalue the qualification process and reduce competition quality.<sup about="#mwt36" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-8" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-8"}}'><a href="#cite_note-8" id="mwSg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwSw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTA">[</span>7<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwTQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwTg">UEFA initially boycotted FIFA events over the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_Forward_Enterprise" title="FIFA Forward Enterprise" id="mwTw">FIFA Forward Enterprise</a> proposal, but provisionally suspended the boycott after receiving written assurance the proposal was permanently cancelled. The UEFA Executive Committee has indicated that they will reinstate the boycott if deemed necessary.<sup about="#mwt39" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-9" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-9"}}'><a href="#cite_note-9" id="mwUA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwUQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUg">[</span>8<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwUw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="2" id="mwVA" aria-labelledby="Trophy"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Trophy">Trophy</h2></div>
<p id="mwVQ">In a 12 October 2026 <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Instagram" title="Instagram" id="mwVg">Instagram</a> post, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_Museum" title="FIFA Museum" id="mwVw">FIFA Museum</a> in Zurich announced that the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_World_Cup_Trophy" title="FIFA World Cup Trophy" id="mwWA">FIFA World Cup Trophy</a>, the trophy for the competition in every edition since 1974, would not be displayed on 14 and 15 October, instead being replaced with the "FIFA World Cup 2030 Winner's Trophy" during those two days.<sup about="#mwt42" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-10" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-10"}}'><a href="#cite_note-10" id="mwWQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwWg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwWw">[</span>9<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwXA">]</span></span></a></sup> While the exact specifications of the FIFA World Cup 2030 Winner's Trophy haven't been announced, a similar commemorative trophy was created for the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Copa_América_Centenario" title="Copa América Centenario" id="mwXQ">Copa América Centenario</a> in 2016 to celebrate the Copa América's centenary.<sup about="#mwt45" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-11" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-11"}}'><a href="#cite_note-11" id="mwXg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwXw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwYA">[</span>10<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwYQ">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="3" id="mwYg" aria-labelledby="Host_selection"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Host_selection">Host selection</h2></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt46" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwYw" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Further","href":"./Template:Further"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"2030 FIFA World Cup bids"},"2":{"wt":"Morocco–Portugal–Spain 2030 FIFA World Cup bid"},"3":{"wt":"Uruguay–Argentina–Chile–Paraguay 2030 FIFA World Cup bid"},"4":{"wt":"Bulgaria–Greece–Romania–Serbia UEFA Euro 2028 and 2030 FIFA World Cup bid"}},"i":0}}]}'><style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1368532237" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" about="#mwt47" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Hatnote/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'>.mw-parser-output .hatnote{font-style:italic}.mw-parser-output div.hatnote{padding-left:1.6em;margin-bottom:0.5em}.mw-parser-output .hatnote i{font-style:normal}.mw-parser-output .hatnote+.mw-empty-elt+.hatnote{margin-top:-0.5em}@media print{body.ns-0 .mw-parser-output .hatnote{display:none!important}}</style></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt46" id="mwZA">Further information: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2030_FIFA_World_Cup_bids" title="2030 FIFA World Cup bids">2030 FIFA World Cup bids</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Morocco–Portugal–Spain_2030_FIFA_World_Cup_bid" title="Morocco–Portugal–Spain 2030 FIFA World Cup bid">Morocco–Portugal–Spain 2030 FIFA World Cup bid</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Uruguay–Argentina–Chile–Paraguay_2030_FIFA_World_Cup_bid" title="Uruguay–Argentina–Chile–Paraguay 2030 FIFA World Cup bid">Uruguay–Argentina–Chile–Paraguay 2030 FIFA World Cup bid</a>, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bulgaria–Greece–Romania–Serbia_UEFA_Euro_2028_and_2030_FIFA_World_Cup_bid" title="Bulgaria–Greece–Romania–Serbia UEFA Euro 2028 and 2030 FIFA World Cup bid">Bulgaria–Greece–Romania–Serbia UEFA Euro 2028 and 2030 FIFA World Cup bid</a></div>
<p id="mwZQ">FIFA launched the bidding process in 2022.<sup about="#mwt51" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-12" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-12"}}'><a href="#cite_note-12" id="mwZg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwZw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwaA">[</span>11<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwaQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt54" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-13" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-13"}}'><a href="#cite_note-13" id="mwag"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwaw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwbA">[</span>12<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwbQ">]</span></span></a></sup> Because of the rule preventing countries belonging to confederations that hosted the two preceding tournaments to host the next,<sup about="#mwt57" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-rotation_14-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"rotation"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-rotation-14"}}'><a href="#cite_note-rotation-14" id="mwbg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwbw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwcA">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwcQ">]</span></span></a></sup><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:All_articles_with_failed_verification" about="#mwt48" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwcg" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Failed verification","href":"./Template:Failed_verification"},"params":{"date":{"wt":"July 2026"}},"i":0}}]}'/><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_with_failed_verification_from_July_2026" about="#mwt48"/><sup class="noprint Inline-Template " style="white-space:nowrap;" about="#mwt48" id="mwcw"><span typeof="mw:Entity">[</span><i><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Verifiability" title="Wikipedia:Verifiability"><span title="The material near this tag failed verification of its source citation(s). (July 2026)">failed verification</span></a></i><span typeof="mw:Entity">]</span></sup> members of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Asian_Football_Confederation" title="Asian Football Confederation" id="mwdA">AFC</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CONCACAF" title="CONCACAF" id="mwdQ">CONCACAF</a> could not bid to host the 2030 FIFA World Cup<sup about="#mwt60" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-15" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-15"}}'><a href="#cite_note-15" id="mwdg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwdw"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mweA">[</span>14<span class="cite-bracket" id="mweQ">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt64" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-telegraph.uk_14_Jan_2017_16-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"telegraph.uk 14 Jan 2017"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-telegraph.uk_14_Jan_2017-16"}}'><a href="#cite_note-telegraph.uk_14_Jan_2017-16" id="mweg"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwew"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwfA">[</span>15<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwfQ">]</span></span></a></sup> – AFC as hosts of the World Cup in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2022_FIFA_World_Cup" title="2022 FIFA World Cup" id="mwfg">2022</a> (Qatar), and CONCACAF as hosts in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_FIFA_World_Cup" title="2026 FIFA World Cup" id="mwfw">2026</a> (United States, Canada, and Mexico).</p>

<p id="mwgA">During a friendly match between <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Portugal_national_football_team" title="Portugal national football team" id="mwgQ">Portugal</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spain_national_football_team" title="Spain national football team" id="mwgg">Spain</a> on 7 October 2020, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Portuguese_Football_Federation" title="Portuguese Football Federation" id="mwgw">FPF</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Royal_Spanish_Football_Federation" title="Royal Spanish Football Federation" id="mwhA">RFEF</a> announced their intentions to produce a joint bid for the tournament.<sup about="#mwt67" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-17" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-17"}}'><a href="#cite_note-17" id="mwhQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwhg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwhw">[</span>16<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwiA">]</span></span></a></sup> Almost two years later, on 5 October 2022, the federations held a joint press conference alongside the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ukrainian_Association_of_Football" title="Ukrainian Association of Football" id="mwiQ">Ukrainian Association of Football</a> president <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Andriy_Pavelko" title="Andriy Pavelko" id="mwig">Andriy Pavelko</a>. On 29 November, The Guardian reported the Ukrainian part of the bid was likely dead due to Pavelko's arrest on suspicion of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Embezzlement" title="Embezzlement" id="mwiw">embezzlement</a>.<sup about="#mwt70" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-18" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-18"}}'><a href="#cite_note-18" id="mwjA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwjQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwjg">[</span>17<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwjw">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt73" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-19" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-19"}}'><a href="#cite_note-19" id="mwkA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwkQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwkg">[</span>18<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwkw">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwlA">The <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Royal_Moroccan_Football_Federation" title="Royal Moroccan Football Federation" id="mwlQ">Royal Moroccan Football Federation</a>–failing to secure hosting rights in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_World_Cup_hosts#1994_FIFA_World_Cup" title="FIFA World Cup hosts" class="mw-redirect" id="mwlg">1994</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_World_Cup_hosts#1998_FIFA_World_Cup" title="FIFA World Cup hosts" class="mw-redirect" id="mwlw">1998</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_World_Cup_hosts#2006_FIFA_World_Cup" title="FIFA World Cup hosts" class="mw-redirect" id="mwmA">2006</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_World_Cup_hosts#2010_FIFA_World_Cup" title="FIFA World Cup hosts" class="mw-redirect" id="mwmQ">2010</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Morocco_2026_FIFA_World_Cup_bid" title="Morocco 2026 FIFA World Cup bid" id="mwmg">2026</a>–bid for the sixth time.<sup about="#mwt76" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-20" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-20"}}'><a href="#cite_note-20" id="mwmw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwnA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwnQ">[</span>19<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwng">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt79" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-21" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-21"}}'><a href="#cite_note-21" id="mwnw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwoA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwoQ">[</span>20<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwog">]</span></span></a></sup> On 14 March 2023, King <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mohammed_VI_of_Morocco" title="Mohammed VI of Morocco" id="mwow">Mohammed VI</a> announced that <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Morocco_national_football_team" title="Morocco national football team" id="mwpA">Morocco</a> would join the Portuguese–Spanish bid.<sup about="#mwt82" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-22" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-22"}}'><a href="#cite_note-22" id="mwpQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwpg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwpw">[</span>21<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwqA">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt85" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-23" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-23"}}'><a href="#cite_note-23" id="mwqQ"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwqg"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwqw">[</span>22<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwrA">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwrQ">On 11 December 2024, FIFA confirmed the 2030 World Cup would be jointly hosted by Morocco, Portugal, and Spain. During an extraordinary FIFA Congress meeting, this announcement was made alongside the decision to award the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2034_FIFA_World_Cup" title="2034 FIFA World Cup" id="mwrg">2034 tournament</a> to Saudi Arabia.<sup about="#mwt88" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-24" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-24"}}'><a href="#cite_note-24" id="mwrw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwsA"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwsQ">[</span>23<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwsg">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

id="mw0Q"> </span></div></a></div></div></div><div class="thumbcaption" style="text-align:left">Location of the candidate cities in Morocco, Portugal and Spain to host the 2030 FIFA World Cup.</div></div></div><span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt93"><link rel="mw:PageProp/Category" href="./Category:Articles_containing_OSM_location_maps"/></span>

<p id="mw0g">Prior to finalising the bid book on 31 July 2024, the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Royal_Spanish_Football_Federation" title="Royal Spanish Football Federation" id="mw0w">Royal Spanish Football Federation</a> announced that a proposed 11 stadiums in 9 cities would host matches."<sup about="#mwt98" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-25" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-25"}}'><a href="#cite_note-25" id="mw1A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw1Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw1g">[</span>24<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw1w">]</span></span></a></sup> The host city list was finalised 12 days later. It includes six stadiums in six cities in Morocco, three stadiums in two cities in Portugal, and eleven stadiums in nine cities in Spain for a total of twenty stadiums in seventeen cities.<sup about="#mwt101" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-26" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-26"}}'><a href="#cite_note-26" id="mw2A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw2Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw2g">[</span>25<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw2w">]</span></span></a></sup> A total of twenty-three different venues are under consideration for hosting matches during the tournament.<sup about="#mwt104" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-27" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-27"}}'><a href="#cite_note-27" id="mw3A"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw3Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw3g">[</span>26<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw3w">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mw4A">In April 2025, some residents of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/San_Sebastián" title="San Sebastián" id="mw4Q">San Sebastián</a> wrote to FIFA asking to be removed as a host city amid <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Overtourism" title="Overtourism" id="mw4g">overtourism</a>.<sup about="#mwt107" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-28" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-28"}}'><a href="#cite_note-28" id="mw4w"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw5A"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw5Q">[</span>27<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw5g">]</span></span></a></sup> On 12 July 2025, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Málaga" title="Málaga" id="mw5w">Málaga</a> withdrew due to logistical reasons regarding the renovation of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Estadio_La_Rosaleda" title="Estadio La Rosaleda" id="mw6A">Estadio La Rosaleda</a>.<sup about="#mwt110" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-29" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-29"}}'><a href="#cite_note-29" id="mw6Q"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw6g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw6w">[</span>28<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw7A">]</span></span></a></sup> On 15 March 2026, the mayor of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/A_Coruña" title="A Coruña" id="mw7Q">A Coruña</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Inés_Rey" title="Inés Rey" id="mw7g">Inés Rey</a>, and also the president of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deportivo_de_A_Coruña" title="Deportivo de A Coruña" id="mw7w">Deportivo de A Coruña</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Juan_Carlos_Escotet" title="Juan Carlos Escotet" id="mw8A">Juan Carlos Escotet</a>, announced they would withdraw as host city due to hosting costs despite having plans to upgrade the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Estadio_Riazor" title="Estadio Riazor" id="mw8Q">Estadio de Riazor</a>.<sup about="#mwt113" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-30" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-30"}}'><a href="#cite_note-30" id="mw8g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw8w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw9A">[</span>29<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw9Q">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mw9g">Towards the end of March 2026, it was reported that the Royal Spanish Football Federation had submitted bids for <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nou_Mestalla" title="Nou Mestalla" id="mw9w">Nou Mestalla</a> in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Valencia" title="Valencia" id="mw-A">Valencia</a>, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Balaídos_Stadium" title="Balaídos Stadium" id="mw-Q">Estadio de Balaídos</a> in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vigo" title="Vigo" id="mw-g">Vigo</a>, to replace Málaga and A Coruña's stadiums. Previous bids for these stadiums were turned down by FIFA for exceeding the limit of twenty stadiums per tournament.<sup about="#mwt116" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-31" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-31"}}'><a href="#cite_note-31" id="mw-w"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mw_A"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mw_Q">[</span>30<span class="cite-bracket" id="mw_g">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwAf8">Three South American cities were also selected in the bid book to host the three centenary matches.<sup about="#mwt122" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-32" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-32"}}'><a href="#cite_note-32" id="mwAgA"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAgE"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAgI">[</span>31<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAgM">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<section data-mw-section-id="7" id="mwAmc" aria-labelledby="Format_changes"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading4"><h4 id="Format_changes">Format changes</h4></div>
<p id="mwAmg">Two regional confederations have announced changes to their qualifying format for this tournament:</p>
<!--;=====AFC===== HIDE UNTIL ANNOUNCED
No changes to the qualifying format used for the 2026 FIFA World Cup are expected for the 2030 cycle. The AFC competition calendars indicate that the qualification process will retain the same multi&#x2D;round structure, culminating in a fourth&#x2D;round group stage and a fifth&#x2D;round play&#x2D;off to determine the confederation's representative in the FIFA intercontinental play&#x2D;offs. -->

<section data-mw-section-id="8" id="mwAmk" aria-labelledby="CONCACAF"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading5"><h5 id="CONCACAF">CONCACAF</h5></div>
<p id="mwAmo">On 6 February 2026, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CONCACAF" title="CONCACAF" id="mwAms">CONCACAF</a> announced their qualifying format for the 2030 FIFA World Cup.<sup about="#mwt160" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-37" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-37"}}'><a href="#cite_note-37" id="mwAmw"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAm0"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAm4">[</span>36<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAm8">]</span></span></a></sup></p>
<ul id="mwAnA"><li id="mwAnE">Round 1: The 22 teams ranked 14–35 in the CONCACAF rankings will be drawn into pairs and play a home-and-away matchup during the first half of the September–October 2027 international window with the 11 winners advancing to Round 2.</li>
<li id="mwAnI">Round 2: The top 13 ranked associations in CONCACAF will be joined by the 11 winners from the Round 1 to create six groups of four teams with each facing each other home-and-away. The top two from each group advance to the final round. Round 2 will begin during the second half of the September–October 2027 international window with later matches in November 2027 and March 2028.</li>
<li id="mwAnM">Final round: The 12 teams that advanced from Round 2 will be divided up into three groups of four with each team facing each other home-and-away. The top two teams in each group will automatically qualify for the World Cup while the two better third-placed finishers will qualify for the play-in. The final round will be played in June 2028 and in the 2029 September–October international window.</li>
<li id="mwAnQ">Play-in: The two teams will play each other home-and-away with the aggregate winner qualifying for the FIFA intercontinental play-offs. The two matches will take place in November 2029.</li></ul>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="9" id="mwAnU" aria-labelledby="UEFA"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading5"><h5 id="UEFA">UEFA</h5></div>
<p id="mwAnY">On 20 May 2026, UEFA announced a new qualification format mirroring both the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UEFA_Champions_League" title="UEFA Champions League" id="mwAnc">Champions League</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UEFA_Nations_League" title="UEFA Nations League" id="mwAng">Nations League</a>. A two-tier qualification process will be created where major teams will no longer play minor teams to remove uncompetitive games. Under the new rules, the 36 highest-ranked countries—as determined by the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2028–29_UEFA_Nations_League" title="2028–29 UEFA Nations League" id="mwAnk">2028–29 Nations League</a>—will be drawn into three groups of 12 in League 1. Teams in those groups would each play six games against six different opponents and be ranked in a 12-team league table. The best-ranked teams of each group of League 1 will qualify for the World Cup, with the remaining places allocated via play-offs. The remaining 18 lower-ranked countries will take part in League 2, split into three groups. All teams in both Leagues will play six home-and-away matches and the system of play-offs will involve teams from both Leagues—those who fall short of direct qualification in League 1 along with top teams from League 2. More details are expected to be released in late 2026 or early 2027.<sup about="#mwt163" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-38" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-38"}}'><a href="#cite_note-38" id="mwAno"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAns"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAnw">[</span>37<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAn0">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt166" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-39" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-39"}}'><a href="#cite_note-39" id="mwAn4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAn8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoA">[</span>38<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoE">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt169" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-40" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-40"}}'><a href="#cite_note-40" id="mwAoI"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAoM"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoQ">[</span>39<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAoU">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section></section></section><section data-mw-section-id="10" id="mwAoY" aria-labelledby="Marketing"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Marketing">Marketing</h2></div>

<li id="mwAus">United Kingdom – <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/BBC" title="BBC" id="mwAuw">BBC</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ITV_(TV_network)" title="ITV (TV network)" id="mwAu0">ITV</a><sup about="#mwt218" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-56" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-56"}}'><a href="#cite_note-56" id="mwAu4"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAu8"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvA">[</span>55<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAvE">]</span></span></a></sup></li></ul>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="13" id="mwAy0" aria-labelledby="Controversies"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Controversies">Controversies</h2></div>
<section data-mw-section-id="14" id="mwAy4" aria-labelledby="Manipulation_of_Spanish_venues"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Manipulation_of_Spanish_venues">Manipulation of Spanish venues</h3></div>
<p id="mwAy8">On 23 March 2025, it was reported that the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Royal_Spanish_Football_Federation" title="Royal Spanish Football Federation" id="mwAzA">Royal Spanish Football Federation</a> manipulated the classification scores when choosing the venues in order to exclude <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Balaídos_Stadium" title="Balaídos Stadium" id="mwAzE">Balaídos Stadium</a> and include <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anoeta_Stadium" title="Anoeta Stadium" id="mwAzI">Anoeta Stadium</a>.<sup about="#mwt245" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-64" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-64"}}'><a href="#cite_note-64" id="mwAzM"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAzQ"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAzU">[</span>63<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAzY">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="15" id="mwAzc" aria-labelledby="Inclusion_of_centenary_match_hosts"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Inclusion_of_centenary_match_hosts">Inclusion of centenary match hosts</h3></div>
<p id="mwAzg">With the FIFA rotation system, which prevents continental associations from hosting consecutive or near-consecutive World Cups,<sup about="#mwt246" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-rotation_14-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"rotation"}}'><a href="#cite_note-rotation-14" id="mwAzk"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwAzo"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAzs">[</span>13<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwAzw">]</span></span></a></sup> <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CONCACAF" title="CONCACAF" id="mwAz0">CONCACAF</a> (which won the rights to host the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_FIFA_World_Cup" title="2026 FIFA World Cup" id="mwAz4">2026 World Cup</a>), <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CONMEBOL" title="CONMEBOL" id="mwAz8">CONMEBOL</a>, <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UEFA" title="UEFA" id="mwA0A">UEFA</a>, and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Confederation_of_African_Football" title="Confederation of African Football" id="mwA0E">CAF</a> were all unable to bid for the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2034_FIFA_World_Cup" title="2034 FIFA World Cup" id="mwA0I">2034</a>, leaving it open only for the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Asian_Football_Confederation" title="Asian Football Confederation" id="mwA0M">AFC</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Oceania_Football_Confederation" title="Oceania Football Confederation" id="mwA0Q">OFC</a>. This led to accusations that FIFA intentionally selected these countries, especially the centenary match hosts in the CONMEBOL region, to ensure that <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saudi_Arabia" title="Saudi Arabia" id="mwA0U">Saudi Arabia</a> would win <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Saudi_Arabia_2034_FIFA_World_Cup_bid" title="Saudi Arabia 2034 FIFA World Cup bid" id="mwA0Y">its bid for the 2034 tournament</a> unopposed.<sup about="#mwt249" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-65" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-65"}}'><a href="#cite_note-65" id="mwA0c"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA0g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA0k">[</span>64<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA0o">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt252" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-66" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-66"}}'><a href="#cite_note-66" id="mwA0s"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA0w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA00">[</span>65<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA04">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="16" id="mwA08" aria-labelledby="Animal_welfare_concerns"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Animal_welfare_concerns">Animal welfare concerns</h3></div>
<p id="mwA1A">Animal rights organisations have accused Morocco of killing stray dogs ahead of its co-hosting of the FIFA World Cup. These groups estimate the stray dog population at 3<span typeof="mw:Entity" id="mwA1E"> </span>million and have alleged that methods used include poisoning and shooting.<sup about="#mwt255" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-67" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-67"}}'><a href="#cite_note-67" id="mwA1I"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA1M"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA1Q">[</span>66<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA1U">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt258" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-newsCull_68-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"newsCull"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-newsCull-68"}}'><a href="#cite_note-newsCull-68" id="mwA1Y"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA1c"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA1g">[</span>67<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA1k">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwA1o">In response, Moroccan authorities adopted Law 19-25 in 2025, which established a legal framework for the management of stray animals. The law introduced measures such as sterilisation, vaccination, identification, and the use of shelters, and restricted the killing of stray animals.<sup about="#mwt261" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-69" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-69"}}'><a href="#cite_note-69" id="mwA1s"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA1w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA10">[</span>68<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA14">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt264" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-70" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-70"}}'><a href="#cite_note-70" id="mwA18"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA2A"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA2E">[</span>69<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA2I">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="17" id="mwA2M" aria-labelledby="Moroccan_occupation_of_Western_Sahara"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Moroccan_occupation_of_Western_Sahara">Moroccan occupation of Western Sahara</h3></div>
<p id="mwA2Q"><a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Western_Sahara" title="Western Sahara" id="mwA2U">Western Sahara</a> is a non-self-governing territory that has been <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moroccan-occupied_Western_Sahara" title="Moroccan-occupied Western Sahara" id="mwA2Y">under Moroccan military occupation</a> since 1975. Analysts expect that Morocco will use the World Cup to bolster its claim over Western Sahara,<sup about="#mwt267" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-71" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-71"}}'><a href="#cite_note-71" id="mwA2c"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA2g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA2k">[</span>70<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA2o">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt270" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-72" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-72"}}'><a href="#cite_note-72" id="mwA2s"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA2w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA20">[</span>71<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA24">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt273" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-73" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-73"}}'><a href="#cite_note-73" id="mwA28"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA3A"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3E">[</span>72<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3I">]</span></span></a></sup> such as through <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sportswashing" title="Sportswashing" id="mwA3M">sportswashing</a>.<sup about="#mwt276" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-74" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-74"}}'><a href="#cite_note-74" id="mwA3Q"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA3U"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3Y">[</span>73<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3c">]</span></span></a></sup> Morocco initially planned to build a stadium in <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dakhla,_Western_Sahara" title="Dakhla, Western Sahara" id="mwA3g">Dakhla</a> for the World Cup,<sup about="#mwt279" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-75" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-75"}}'><a href="#cite_note-75" id="mwA3k"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA3o"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3s">[</span>74<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA3w">]</span></span></a></sup> though ultimately no matches will take place in the territory.<sup about="#mwt282" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ElInd_76-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ElInd"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-ElInd-76"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ElInd-76" id="mwA30"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA34"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA38">[</span>75<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA4A">]</span></span></a></sup> Morocco regularly expels journalists who bring attention towards the occupation.<sup about="#mwt285" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-77" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-77"}}'><a href="#cite_note-77" id="mwA4E"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA4I"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA4M">[</span>76<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA4Q">]</span></span></a></sup> It is doubling its green energy production in the territory to meet its own demands for the World Cup.<sup about="#mwt288" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-78" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-78"}}'><a href="#cite_note-78" id="mwA4U"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA4Y"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA4c">[</span>77<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA4g">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

<p id="mwA4k">In their 2030 bid document, Morocco, Spain, and Portugal included a map that depicted Western Sahara as Moroccan territory; FIFA's bid evaluation report did not reproduce this map.<sup about="#mwt289" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-ElInd_76-1" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"ElInd"}}'><a href="#cite_note-ElInd-76" id="mwA4o"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA4s"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA4w">[</span>75<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA40">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="18" id="mwA44" aria-labelledby="Gen_Z_212_protests"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Gen_Z_212_protests">Gen Z 212 protests</h3></div>
<span class="mw-empty-elt" about="#mwt290" typeof="mw:Transclusion" id="mwA48" data-mw='{"parts":[{"template":{"target":{"wt":"Main","href":"./Template:Main"},"params":{"1":{"wt":"2025 Moroccan Gen Z protests"}},"i":0}}]}'><link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1368532237" about="#mwt291" typeof="mw:Extension/templatestyles" data-mw='{"name":"templatestyles","attrs":{"src":"Module:Hatnote/styles.css"},"body":{"extsrc":""}}'/></span><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable" about="#mwt290" id="mwA5A">Main article: <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Moroccan_Gen_Z_protests" title="2025 Moroccan Gen Z protests">2025 Moroccan Gen Z protests</a></div>
<p id="mwA5E">In late September 2025, a series of protests erupted in several cities in Morocco led by the youth movement known as Gen Z 212.<sup about="#mwt294" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:15_79-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":15"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:15-79"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:15-79" id="mwA5I"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA5M"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA5Q">[</span>78<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA5U">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt297" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-80" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-80"}}'><a href="#cite_note-80" id="mwA5Y"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA5c"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA5g">[</span>79<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA5k">]</span></span></a></sup> The protests were sparked by the deterioration of the country's health and education systems and the government's excessive spending on sports infrastructure in preparation for hosting the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2025_Africa_Cup_of_Nations" title="2025 Africa Cup of Nations" id="mwA5o">2025 Africa Cup of Nations</a> and the upcoming 2030 FIFA World Cup.<sup about="#mwt300" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-CNN_81-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"CNN"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-CNN-81"}}'><a href="#cite_note-CNN-81" id="mwA5s"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA5w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA50">[</span>80<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA54">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt303" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-AlJazeera_82-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"AlJazeera"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-AlJazeera-82"}}'><a href="#cite_note-AlJazeera-82" id="mwA58"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA6A"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA6E">[</span>81<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA6I">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt306" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-Reuters_83-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"Reuters"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-Reuters-83"}}'><a href="#cite_note-Reuters-83" id="mwA6M"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA6Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA6U">[</span>82<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA6Y">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt309" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-:11_84-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":":11"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-:11-84"}}'><a href="#cite_note-:11-84" id="mwA6c"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA6g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA6k">[</span>83<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA6o">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="19" id="mwA6s" aria-labelledby="UEFA_boycott"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="UEFA_boycott">UEFA boycott</h3></div>
<p id="mwA6w">On 30 July 2026, following FIFA's announcement of <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FIFA_Forward_Enterprise" title="FIFA Forward Enterprise" id="mwA60">FIFA Forward Enterprise</a> (a proposal to sell stakes in FIFA's tournament operations, including for the World Cup, to private investors), all 55 <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UEFA" title="UEFA" id="mwA64">UEFA</a> member associations agreed on an indefinite boycott of FIFA competitions until the proposal was shelved and assurances were made that such a proposal would not be tabulated again. Two of the 2030 FIFA World Cup hosts – Spain and Portugal – are involved in the boycott.<sup about="#mwt312" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-FFEBBC_85-0" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{"name":"FFEBBC"},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-FFEBBC-85"}}'><a href="#cite_note-FFEBBC-85" id="mwA68"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA7A"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA7E">[</span>84<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA7I">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt315" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-86" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-86"}}'><a href="#cite_note-86" id="mwA7M"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA7Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA7U">[</span>85<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA7Y">]</span></span></a></sup> On 31 July, FIFA scrapped the proposal,<sup about="#mwt318" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-87" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-87"}}'><a href="#cite_note-87" id="mwA7c"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA7g"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA7k">[</span>86<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA7o">]</span></span></a></sup> although UEFA stated that, regardless, it no longer trusts FIFA's governance.<sup about="#mwt321" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-88" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-88"}}'><a href="#cite_note-88" id="mwA7s"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA7w"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA70">[</span>87<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA74">]</span></span></a></sup> After receiving written assurances from FIFA that similar plans would not be explored in the future, UEFA ended the boycott on 26 August.<sup about="#mwt324" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-89" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-89"}}'><a href="#cite_note-89" id="mwA78"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA8A"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA8E">[</span>88<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA8I">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt327" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-90" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-90"}}'><a href="#cite_note-90" id="mwA8M"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA8Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA8U">[</span>89<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA8Y">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="20" id="mwA8c" aria-labelledby="Calls_for_Morocco's_removal_as_a_host"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Calls_for_Morocco's_removal_as_a_host"><span id="Calls_for_Morocco.27s_removal_as_a_host" typeof="mw:FallbackId"></span>Calls for Morocco's removal as a host</h3></div>
<p id="mwA8g">On 5 August 2026, Portuguese left-wing political party <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/LIVRE" title="LIVRE" id="mwA8k">LIVRE</a> called for Morocco to be removed from hosting the 2030 FIFA World Cup following the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2026_Ceuta_migrant_crisis" title="2026 Ceuta migrant crisis" id="mwA8o">Ceuta migration crisis</a>. The party urged the Portuguese and Spanish governments, as well as the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/European_Union" title="European Union" id="mwA8s">European Union</a> and <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/UEFA" title="UEFA" id="mwA8w">UEFA</a>, to support a hosting arrangement involving only Portugal and Spain, citing Morocco's handling of migration at the <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ceuta" title="Ceuta" id="mwA80">Ceuta</a> border and broader human rights concerns.<sup about="#mwt330" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-91" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-91"}}'><a href="#cite_note-91" id="mwA84"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA88"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA9A">[</span>90<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA9E">]</span></span></a></sup> LIVRE spokesperson <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jorge_Pinto" title="Jorge Pinto" id="mwA9I">Jorge Pinto</a> described the crisis as "the last straw" and questioned FIFA's governance and the transparency of its host selection process. The party had also previously criticized Morocco's involvement in the bid over its policies regarding Western Sahara.<sup about="#mwt333" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-92" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-92"}}'><a href="#cite_note-92" id="mwA9M"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA9Q"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA9U">[</span>91<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA9Y">]</span></span></a></sup> On the same day, Spanish left-wing political party and member of the Spanish government <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sumar_(electoral_platform)" title="Sumar (electoral platform)" id="mwA9c">Sumar</a> registered a non-binding motion calling on the government to discuss with the Spanish Royal Football Federation the need to "review and reevaluate" the current joint organization model with Morocco amid criticism over "grave breaches" on international agreements and human rights violations.<sup about="#mwt336" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-93" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-93"}}'><a href="#cite_note-93" id="mwA9g"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA9k"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA9o">[</span>92<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA9s">]</span></span></a></sup><sup about="#mwt339" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-94" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-94"}}'><a href="#cite_note-94" id="mwA9w"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA90"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA94">[</span>93<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA98">]</span></span></a></sup> Later, far-left party <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Podemos_(Spanish_political_party)" title="Podemos (Spanish political party)" id="mwA-A">Podemos</a> echoed the call to exclude Morocco from the organization after stating that the country "attacked Spain’s sovereignty".<sup about="#mwt342" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-95" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-95"}}'><a href="#cite_note-95" id="mwA-E"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA-I"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA-M">[</span>94<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA-Q">]</span></span></a></sup> On 6 August, far-right party <a rel="mw:WikiLink" href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vox_(political_party)" title="Vox (political party)" id="mwA-U">Vox</a> submitted another non-binding motion asking for the same.<sup about="#mwt345" class="mw-ref reference" id="cite_ref-96" rel="dc:references" typeof="mw:Extension/ref" data-mw='{"name":"ref","attrs":{},"body":{"id":"mw-reference-text-cite_note-96"}}'><a href="#cite_note-96" id="mwA-Y"><span class="mw-reflink-text" id="mwA-c"><span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA-g">[</span>95<span class="cite-bracket" id="mwA-k">]</span></span></a></sup></p>

</section></section><section data-mw-section-id="21" id="mwA-o" aria-labelledby="Notes"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Notes">Notes</h2></div>

</section><section data-mw-section-id="22" id="mwA_I" aria-labelledby="References"><div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="References">References</h2></div>
